Bulk Resin Charms & Flatback Cabochons

Source bulk resin charms and flatback cabochons for slime, decoden, bag charms and DIY kits. Browse food, animal and miniature styles; request B2B pricing.

What are resin flatback charms? Solid decorative pieces with a flat reverse side for glue-on applications such as decoden, phone cases, hair clips and slime. Browse food, animal and miniature themes, then confirm dimensions, backing style, packing and any required test-document scope by SKU.

Resin Charms, Flatback Charms & Decoden Designs

Compare resin charms wholesale by design and attachment. For decoden charms, start with flatback cabochons and check the reverse side and adhesive on a sample. For hanging designs, confirm the hole or fitting on the selected SKU. Review backing and packing checks.

Shortlist RW22372 chocolate chip cookie flatback cabochons and RW4252 jelly bean resin charms: 12 Ã— 22 mm, 100 pieces per bag. Use the decoden supplies guide to plan matching slices and fillers. Pack quantity is separate from MOQ.
